# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in <Operations> with a specfication.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
A company is comprised of two to eight departments. Each department has an ID and email. A department hires employees for certain projects. Employees working on projects can be temporary employees or permanent employees. Each employee is identified by a name, email, employee ID, employee number. Projects can be of types - production projects, research projects, education projects, and community projects. All projects have a title, description, budget amount, and deadline. In addition, each education project and community project are associated with one funding group. The funding group can be of types - private group, government group, or mixed group. The production projects are characterized by a site code.

functional requirement：
1.Total budget of all projects in a company.Calculate the sum of the budget amounts of all projects in all departments of a company. 

2.Average budget of all projects in a department.Calculate the average budget amount of all projects in a given department.

3.Count the distinct number of employees who work on at least one production projects in the company. An employee working on multiple production projects is counted only once.

4.Funding group type of all community projects in a department.Retrieve the funding group type of all community projects within a given department.

5.Manage departments in a company. Ensure that the company always has between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Allow adding a new department with unique name only if the current number of departments is less than 8, and allow removing an existing department by name only if the current number of departments is greater than 2; reject any operation that would violate these constraints. When the number of departments exceeds the maximum limit or falls below the minimum limit, throw an IllegalStateException; when the department name is duplicate, null, empty, or the target department to remove does not exist, throw an IllegalArgumentException.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title project Model

class CommunityProject {
    fundingGroup : FundingGroup
}
class Company {
    departments : List<Department>
    addDepartment(department : Department) : void
    calculateTotalBudget() : double
    countEmployeesInProductionProjects() : int
    removeDepartment(name : String) : String
}
class Department {
    ID : String
    email : String
    company : Company
    employees : List<Employee>
    projects : List<Project>
    calculateAverageBudget() : double
    getFundingGroupTypeCommunityProjects() : List<FundingGroupType>
}
class EducationProject {
    fundingGroup : FundingGroup
}
class Employee {
    ID : String
    email : String
    name : String
    number : String
    type : EmployeeType
    department : Department
}
class FundingGroup {
    name : String
    type : FundingGroupType
    communityProjects : List<CommunityProject>
    educationProjects : List<EducationProject>
}
class ProductionProject {
    siteCode : String
}
abstract class Project {
    budget : double
    deadline : EDate
    description : String
    title : String
    department : Department
    workingEmployees : List<Employee>
}
class ResearchProject {
}
enum EmployeeType {
    TEMPORARY
    PERMANENT
}
enum FundingGroupType {
    PRIVATE
    GOVERNMENT
    MIXED
}

CommunityProject --|> Project
CommunityProject --> FundingGroup : fundingGroup  'opposite: communityProjects
Company *-- "*" Department : departments  'opposite: company
Department --> Company : company  'opposite: departments
Department *-- "*" Employee : employees  'opposite: department
Department *-- "*" Project : projects  'opposite: department
EducationProject --|> Project
EducationProject --> FundingGroup : fundingGroup  'opposite: educationProjects
Employee --> Department : department  'opposite: employees
FundingGroup --> "*" CommunityProject : communityProjects  'opposite: fundingGroup
FundingGroup --> "*" EducationProject : educationProjects  'opposite: fundingGroup
ProductionProject --|> Project
Project --> Department : department  'opposite: projects
Project --> "*" Employee : workingEmployees
ResearchProject --|> Project
@enduml
```

# Requirement
```text
A company is comprised of two to eight departments. Each department has an ID and email. A department hires employees for certain projects. Employees working on projects can be temporary employees or permanent employees. Each employee is identified by a name, email, employee ID, employee number. Projects can be of types - production projects, research projects, education projects, and community projects. All projects have a title, description, budget amount, and deadline. In addition, each education project and community project are associated with one funding group. The funding group can be of types - private group, government group, or mixed group. The production projects are characterized by a site code.

functional requirement：
1.Total budget of all projects in a company.Calculate the sum of the budget amounts of all projects in all departments of a company. 

2.Average budget of all projects in a department.Calculate the average budget amount of all projects in a given department.

3.Count the distinct number of employees who work on at least one production projects in the company. An employee working on multiple production projects is counted only once.

4.Funding group type of all community projects in a department.Retrieve the funding group type of all community projects within a given department.

5.Manage departments in a company. Ensure that the company always has between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Allow adding a new department with unique name only if the current number of departments is less than 8, and allow removing an existing department by name only if the current number of departments is greater than 2; reject any operation that would violate these constraints. When the number of departments exceeds the maximum limit or falls below the minimum limit, throw an IllegalStateException; when the department name is duplicate, null, empty, or the target department to remove does not exist, throw an IllegalArgumentException.
```

2. Algorithm:
   employeeSet = new HashSet<String>()
   for each department in departments:
       for each project in department.projects:
           if project is ProductionProject:
               for each employee in project.workingEmployees:
                   employeeSet.add(employee.ID)
   return employeeSet.size()

2. Algorithm:
   if (department == null || department.getName() == null || department.getName().trim().isEmpty()) {
       throw new IllegalArgumentException("Department or its name is null/empty");
   }
   if (departments.stream().anyMatch(d -> d.getName().equals(department.getName()))) {

   if (departments.size() >= 8) {
       throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot exceed maximum of 8 departments");

   department.setCompany(this);

8. Post-condition: The department is added to the company's department list, and the department's company reference is set to this company. The company now has one more department.

Company::removeDepartment
1. Summary: Removes an existing department by name while enforcing the constraint that the company must always have between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Validates that the department exists.
2. Algorithm:
   if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty()) {
       throw new IllegalArgumentException("Department name cannot be null or empty");
   }
   if (departments.size() <= 2) {
       throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot go below minimum of 2 departments");
   }
   department = departments.stream()
       .filter(d -> d.getName().equals(name))
       .findFirst()
       .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Department not found: " + name));
   departments.remove(department);

# Requirement
```text
A company is comprised of two to eight departments. Each department has an ID and email. A department hires employees for certain projects. Employees working on projects can be temporary employees or permanent employees. Each employee is identified by a name, email, employee ID, employee number. Projects can be of types - production projects, research projects, education projects, and community projects. All projects have a title, description, budget amount, and deadline. In addition, each education project and community project are associated with one funding group. The funding group can be of types - private group, government group, or mixed group. The production projects are characterized by a site code.

functional requirement：
1.Total budget of all projects in a company.Calculate the sum of the budget amounts of all projects in all departments of a company. 

2.Average budget of all projects in a department.Calculate the average budget amount of all projects in a given department.

3.Count the distinct number of employees who work on at least one production projects in the company. An employee working on multiple production projects is counted only once.

4.Funding group type of all community projects in a department.Retrieve the funding group type of all community projects within a given department.

5.Manage departments in a company. Ensure that the company always has between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Allow adding a new department with unique name only if the current number of departments is less than 8, and allow removing an existing department by name only if the current number of departments is greater than 2; reject any operation that would violate these constraints. When the number of departments exceeds the maximum limit or falls below the minimum limit, throw an IllegalStateException; when the department name is duplicate, null, empty, or the target department to remove does not exist, throw an IllegalArgumentException.
```

# Operation Specifications
Company::countEmployeesInProductionProjects
1. Summary: Counts the distinct number of employees who work on at least one production project in the company. An employee working on multiple production projects is counted only once.
2. Algorithm:
   employeeSet = new HashSet<String>()
   for each department in departments:
       for each project in department.projects:
           if project is ProductionProject:
               for each employee in project.workingEmployees:
                   employeeSet.add(employee.ID)
   return employeeSet.size()
3. Related Features: departments (List<Department>), department.projects (List<Project>), project.workingEmployees (List<Employee>), Employee.ID (String)
4. Related Classes: Department, Project, ProductionProject, Employee
5. Input: None
6. Output: int – the count of distinct employees working on at least one production project. Must be >= 0.
7. Pre-condition: None
8. Post-condition: Returns the distinct employee count. No state change.

Company::addDepartment
1. Summary: Adds a new department to the company while enforcing the constraint that the company must always have between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Validates that the department name is unique, non-null, and non-empty.
2. Algorithm:
   if (department == null || department.getName() == null || department.getName().trim().isEmpty()) {
       throw new IllegalArgumentException("Department or its name is null/empty");
   }
   if (departments.stream().anyMatch(d -> d.getName().equals(department.getName()))) {
       throw new IllegalArgumentException("Duplicate department name");
   }
   if (departments.size() >= 8) {
       throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot exceed maximum of 8 departments");
   }
   departments.add(department);
   department.setCompany(this);
3. Related Features: departments (List<Department>), company reference in Department
4. Related Classes: Department
5. Input: department : Department – the department object to add. Must have a non-null, non-empty, unique name.
6. Output: void
7. Pre-condition: The department object must not be null; its name must not be null, empty, or duplicate of existing department names.
8. Post-condition: The department is added to the company's department list, and the department's company reference is set to this company. The company now has one more department.

Company::removeDepartment
1. Summary: Removes an existing department by name while enforcing the constraint that the company must always have between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Validates that the department exists.
2. Algorithm:
   if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty()) {
       throw new IllegalArgumentException("Department name cannot be null or empty");
   }
   if (departments.size() <= 2) {
       throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot go below minimum of 2 departments");
   }
   department = departments.stream()
       .filter(d -> d.getName().equals(name))
       .findFirst()
       .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Department not found: " + name));
   departments.remove(department);
   return name;
3. Related Features: departments (List<Department>)
4. Related Classes: Department
5. Input: name : String – the name (ID) of the department to remove. Must not be null or empty, and must match an existing department name.
6. Output: String – the name of the removed department.
7. Pre-condition: The company must have more than 2 departments; the provided name must be non-null, non-empty, and match an existing department.
8. Post-condition: The department is removed from the company's department list. The company now has one fewer department.

Company::calculateTotalBudget
1. Summary: Calculates the sum of the budget amounts of all projects across all departments in the company.
2. Algorithm:
   total = 0.0
   for each department in departments:
       for each project in department.projects:
           total += project.budget
   return total
3. Related Features: departments (List<Department>), department.projects (List<Project>), project.budget (double)
4. Related Classes: Department, Project
5. Input: None
6. Output: double – the total budget sum of all projects in the company. Must be >= 0.
7. Pre-condition: None
8. Post-condition: Returns the sum of all project budgets. No state change.

- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

  public void addDepartment(final Department department)
  {
    if (department == null) {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Department must not be null");
    }
    String name = department.getID();
    if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty()) {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Department name must be non-null and non-empty");
    }
    if (getDepartments().size() >= 8) {
      throw new IllegalStateException("Company cannot have more than 8 departments");
    }
    for (Department existing : getDepartments()) {
      if (name.equals(existing.getID())) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Department name must be unique");
      }
    }
    getDepartments().add(department);
    department.setCompany(this);
  }

    }
    if (getDepartments().size() <= 2) {
      throw new IllegalStateException("Company must have at least 2 departments");

    Department found = null;
    for (Department department : getDepartments()) {
      if (name.equals(department.getID())) {
        found = department;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (found == null) {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Department with name '" + name + "' not found");
    }
    getDepartments().remove(found);
    return found.getID();

    }
    getDepartments().add(department);
    department.setCompany(this);
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Removes an existing department by name while enforcing the constraint that the company must always have between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Validates that the department exists.
   * Algorithm:
   * Related Features: departments (List<Department>)
   * Related Classes: Department
   * Input: name : String – the name (ID) of the department to remove. Must not be null or empty, and must match an existing department name.
   * Output: String – the name of the removed department.
   * Pre-condition: The company must have more than 2 departments; the provided name must be non-null, non-empty, and match an existing department.
   * Post-condition: The department is removed from the company's department list. The company now has one fewer department.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public String removeDepartment(final String name)
  {
    if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty()) {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Department name must be non-null and non-empty");
    }
    if (getDepartments().size() <= 2) {
      throw new IllegalStateException("Company must have at least 2 departments");
    }
    Department found = null;
    for (Department department : getDepartments()) {
      if (name.equals(department.getID())) {
        found = department;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (found == null) {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Department with name '" + name + "' not found");
    }
    getDepartments().remove(found);
    return found.getID();
  }

- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
  public double calculateAverageBudget()
  {
    EList<Project> projectList = getProjects();
    if (projectList.isEmpty()) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    double sum = 0.0;
    for (Project project : projectList) {
        sum += project.getBudget();
    }
    return sum / projectList.size();
  }

  public EList<FundingGroupType> getFundingGroupTypeCommunityProjects()
  {
    EList<FundingGroupType> result = new BasicEList<FundingGroupType>();
    for (Project project : getProjects()) {
        if (project instanceof CommunityProject) {
            CommunityProject communityProject = (CommunityProject) project;
            FundingGroup fundingGroup = communityProject.getFundingGroup();
            if (fundingGroup != null) {
                result.add(fundingGroup.getType());
            }
        }
    }
    return result;
  }
```

    }
    return sum / projectList.size();
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Retrieves the funding group type of all community projects within this department.
   * 2. Algorithm:
   *    result = new ArrayList<FundingGroupType>()
   *    for each project in projects:
   *        if project is CommunityProject:
   *            result.add(project.fundingGroup.type)
   *    return result
   * 3. Related Features: projects (List<Project>), CommunityProject.fundingGroup (FundingGroup), FundingGroup.type (FundingGroupType)
   * 4. Related Classes: Project, CommunityProject, FundingGroup, FundingGroupType
   * 5. Input: None
   * 6. Output: List<FundingGroupType> – a list of funding group types of all community projects in this department. May be empty if no community projects exist.
   * 7. Pre-condition: None
   * 8. Post-condition: Returns the list of funding group types. No state change.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model kind="operation"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<FundingGroupType> getFundingGroupTypeCommunityProjects()
  {
    EList<FundingGroupType> result = new BasicEList<FundingGroupType>();
    for (Project project : getProjects()) {
        if (project instanceof CommunityProject) {
            CommunityProject communityProject = (CommunityProject) project;
            result.add(communityProject.getFundingGroup().getType());
        }
    }
    return result;
  }

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in <Operations> with a specfication.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
A company is comprised of two to eight departments. Each department has an ID and email. A department hires employees for certain projects. Employees working on projects can be temporary employees or permanent employees. Each employee is identified by a name, email, employee ID, employee number. Projects can be of types - production projects, research projects, education projects, and community projects. All projects have a title, description, budget amount, and deadline. In addition, each education project and community project are associated with one funding group. The funding group can be of types - private group, government group, or mixed group. The production projects are characterized by a site code.

functional requirement：
1.Total budget of all projects in a company.Calculate the sum of the budget amounts of all projects in all departments of a company. 

2.Average budget of all projects in a department.Calculate the average budget amount of all projects in a given department.

3.Count the distinct number of employees who work on at least one production projects in the company. An employee working on multiple production projects is counted only once.

4.Funding group type of all community projects in a department.Retrieve the funding group type of all community projects within a given department.

5.Manage departments in a company. Ensure that the company always has between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Allow adding a new department with unique name only if the current number of departments is less than 8, and allow removing an existing department by name only if the current number of departments is greater than 2; reject any operation that would violate these constraints. When the number of departments exceeds the maximum limit or falls below the minimum limit, throw an IllegalStateException; when the department name is duplicate, null, empty, or the target department to remove does not exist, throw an IllegalArgumentException.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title project Model

class CommunityProject {
    fundingGroup : FundingGroup
}
class Company {
    departments : List<Department>
    addDepartment(department : Department) : void
    calculateTotalBudget() : double
    countEmployeesInProductionProjects() : int
    removeDepartment(name : String) : String
}
class Department {
    ID : String
    email : String
    company : Company
    employees : List<Employee>
    projects : List<Project>
    calculateAverageBudget() : double
    getFundingGroupTypeCommunityProjects() : List<FundingGroupType>
}
class EducationProject {
    fundingGroup : FundingGroup
}
class Employee {
    ID : String
    email : String
    name : String
    number : String
    type : EmployeeType
    department : Department
}
class FundingGroup {
    name : String
    type : FundingGroupType
    communityProjects : List<CommunityProject>
    educationProjects : List<EducationProject>
}
class ProductionProject {
    siteCode : String
}
abstract class Project {
    budget : double
    deadline : EDate
    description : String
    title : String
    department : Department
    workingEmployees : List<Employee>
}
class ResearchProject {
}
enum EmployeeType {
    TEMPORARY
    PERMANENT
}
enum FundingGroupType {
    PRIVATE
    GOVERNMENT
    MIXED
}

CommunityProject --|> Project
CommunityProject --> FundingGroup : fundingGroup  'opposite: communityProjects
Company *-- "*" Department : departments  'opposite: company
Department --> Company : company  'opposite: departments
Department *-- "*" Employee : employees  'opposite: department
Department *-- "*" Project : projects  'opposite: department
EducationProject --|> Project
EducationProject --> FundingGroup : fundingGroup  'opposite: educationProjects
Employee --> Department : department  'opposite: employees
FundingGroup --> "*" CommunityProject : communityProjects  'opposite: fundingGroup
FundingGroup --> "*" EducationProject : educationProjects  'opposite: fundingGroup
ProductionProject --|> Project
Project --> Department : department  'opposite: projects
Project --> "*" Employee : workingEmployees
ResearchProject --|> Project
@enduml
```

# Operations
+ Company::addDepartment
+ Company::calculateTotalBudget
+ Company::countEmployeesInProductionProjects
+ Company::removeDepartment
+ Department::calculateAverageBudget
+ Department::getFundingGroupTypeCommunityProjects

4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
A company is comprised of two to eight departments. Each department has an ID and email. A department hires employees for certain projects. Employees working on projects can be temporary employees or permanent employees. Each employee is identified by a name, email, employee ID, employee number. Projects can be of types - production projects, research projects, education projects, and community projects. All projects have a title, description, budget amount, and deadline. In addition, each education project and community project are associated with one funding group. The funding group can be of types - private group, government group, or mixed group. The production projects are characterized by a site code.

functional requirement：
1.Total budget of all projects in a company.Calculate the sum of the budget amounts of all projects in all departments of a company. 

2.Average budget of all projects in a department.Calculate the average budget amount of all projects in a given department.

3.Count the distinct number of employees who work on at least one production projects in the company. An employee working on multiple production projects is counted only once.

4.Funding group type of all community projects in a department.Retrieve the funding group type of all community projects within a given department.

5.Manage departments in a company. Ensure that the company always has between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Allow adding a new department with unique name only if the current number of departments is less than 8, and allow removing an existing department by name only if the current number of departments is greater than 2; reject any operation that would violate these constraints. When the number of departments exceeds the maximum limit or falls below the minimum limit, throw an IllegalStateException; when the department name is duplicate, null, empty, or the target department to remove does not exist, throw an IllegalArgumentException.
```

```plantuml
@startuml

title project Model

class CommunityProject {
    fundingGroup : FundingGroup
}

    departments : List<Department>

    getFundingGroupTypeCommunityProjects() : List<FundingGroupType>

    fundingGroup : FundingGroup
}

    ID : String
    email : String
    name : String
    number : String
    type : EmployeeType
    department : Department
}

    name : String
    type : FundingGroupType
    communityProjects : List<CommunityProject>
    educationProjects : List<EducationProject>
}
class ProductionProject {
    siteCode : String
}
abstract class Project {
    budget : double
    deadline : EDate
    description : String
    title : String
    department : Department
    workingEmployees : List<Employee>
}
class ResearchProject {
}
enum EmployeeType {
    TEMPORARY
    PERMANENT
}
enum FundingGroupType {
    PRIVATE
    GOVERNMENT
    MIXED
}

CommunityProject --|> Project
CommunityProject --> FundingGroup : fundingGroup  'opposite: communityProjects

Employee --> Department : department  'opposite: employees
FundingGroup --> "*" CommunityProject : communityProjects  'opposite: fundingGroup
FundingGroup --> "*" EducationProject : educationProjects  'opposite: fundingGroup
ProductionProject --|> Project
Project --> Department : department  'opposite: projects
Project --> "*" Employee : workingEmployees
ResearchProject --|> Project
@enduml

# Operation Specifications
Department::calculateAverageBudget
Summary: Calculates the average budget amount of all projects in this department.
Algorithm:
  1. If projects list is empty, return 0.0
  2. Initialize sum = 0.0
  3. For each project in projects:
     a. Add project.budget to sum
  4. Return sum / projects.size()
Related Features: projects (List), budget (in Project)
Related Classes: Project
Input: None
Output: double - The average budget of all projects in this department. Returns 0.0 if no projects exist.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns the arithmetic mean of all project budgets in this department.

Department::getFundingGroupTypeCommunityProjects
Summary: Retrieves the funding group type of all community projects within this department.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize an empty List<FundingGroupType> called result
  2. For each project in projects:
     a. If project is an instance of CommunityProject:
        - Add project.fundingGroup.type to result
  3. Return result
Related Features: projects (List), CommunityProject type, fundingGroup (in CommunityProject), type (in FundingGroup)
Related Classes: Project, CommunityProject, FundingGroup, FundingGroupType
Input: None
Output: List<FundingGroupType> - A list of FundingGroupType values for all community projects in this department. Order follows the order of projects in the list.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns a list containing the funding group type for each community project. If no community projects exist, returns an empty list.

4.Funding group type of all community projects in a department.Retrieve the funding group type of all community projects within a given department.

5.Manage departments in a company. Ensure that the company always has between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Allow adding a new department with unique name only if the current number of departments is less than 8, and allow removing an existing department by name only if the current number of departments is greater than 2; reject any operation that would violate these constraints. When the number of departments exceeds the maximum limit or falls below the minimum limit, throw an IllegalStateException; when the department name is duplicate, null, empty, or the target department to remove does not exist, throw an IllegalArgumentException.
```

# Operation Specifications
Company::removeDepartment
Summary: Removes an existing department by name from the company, ensuring the company always maintains between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive).
Algorithm:
  1. Validate name is not null or empty.
  2. Find the department with matching name in departments list.
  3. If not found, throw IllegalArgumentException.
  4. Check that departments.size() > 2 (after removal would be at least 2).
  5. If validation fails, throw appropriate exception.
  6. Remove the department from departments list.
  7. Return the name of the removed department.
Related Features: departments (List)
Related Classes: Department
Input:
  - name: String - The name (ID) of the department to remove. Must not be null or empty.
Output: String - The name of the department that was removed.
Pre-condition:
  - name is not null and not empty
  - A department with the given name exists in departments
  - departments.size() > 2
Post-condition:
  - departments.size() decreases by 1
  - The department with the given name is removed
  - Returns the name of the removed department
Exceptions:
  - IllegalArgumentException: if name is null/empty, or department not found
  - IllegalStateException: if departments.size() <= 2

Company::calculateTotalBudget
Summary: Calculates the sum of the budget amounts of all projects across all departments in the company.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize total = 0.0
  2. For each department in departments:
     a. For each project in department.projects:
        - Add project.budget to total
  3. Return total
Related Features: departments, projects (in Department), budget (in Project)
Related Classes: Department, Project
Input: None
Output: double - The total budget amount of all projects in the company.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns the sum of all project budgets across all departments.

Company::countEmployeesInProductionProjects
Summary: Counts the distinct number of employees who work on at least one production project in the company. An employee working on multiple production projects is counted only once.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize an empty Set<Employee> called productionEmployees
  2. For each department in departments:
     a. For each project in department.projects:
        - If project is an instance of ProductionProject:
          i. For each employee in project.workingEmployees:
             - Add employee to productionEmployees set
  3. Return productionEmployees.size()
Related Features: departments, projects, workingEmployees (in Project), ProductionProject type
Related Classes: Department, Project, ProductionProject, Employee
Input: None
Output: int - The count of distinct employees working on at least one production project.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns the number of distinct employees involved in production projects.

Company::addDepartment
Summary: Adds a new department to the company, ensuring the company always maintains between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive).
Algorithm:
  1. Validate the input department name is not null, empty, or duplicate.
  2. Check that the current number of departments is less than 8.
  3. If validation fails, throw appropriate exception.
  4. Add the department to the departments list.
  5. Set the department's company reference to this company.
Related Features: departments (List), company reference of Department
Related Classes: Department
Input:
  - department: Department object to be added. Must have a non-null, non-empty, unique name.
Output: void
Pre-condition:
  - department is not null
  - department.name is not null and not empty
  - department.name is unique among existing departments
  - departments.size() < 8
Post-condition:
  - departments.size() increases by 1
  - The new department is added to departments list
  - department.company == this
Exceptions:
  - IllegalArgumentException: if department is null, name is null/empty, or name is duplicate
  - IllegalStateException: if departments.size() >= 8

# Requirement
```text
A company is comprised of two to eight departments. Each department has an ID and email. A department hires employees for certain projects. Employees working on projects can be temporary employees or permanent employees. Each employee is identified by a name, email, employee ID, employee number. Projects can be of types - production projects, research projects, education projects, and community projects. All projects have a title, description, budget amount, and deadline. In addition, each education project and community project are associated with one funding group. The funding group can be of types - private group, government group, or mixed group. The production projects are characterized by a site code.

functional requirement：
1.Total budget of all projects in a company.Calculate the sum of the budget amounts of all projects in all departments of a company. 

2.Average budget of all projects in a department.Calculate the average budget amount of all projects in a given department.

3.Count the distinct number of employees who work on at least one production projects in the company. An employee working on multiple production projects is counted only once.

4.Funding group type of all community projects in a department.Retrieve the funding group type of all community projects within a given department.

5.Manage departments in a company. Ensure that the company always has between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive). Allow adding a new department with unique name only if the current number of departments is less than 8, and allow removing an existing department by name only if the current number of departments is greater than 2; reject any operation that would violate these constraints. When the number of departments exceeds the maximum limit or falls below the minimum limit, throw an IllegalStateException; when the department name is duplicate, null, empty, or the target department to remove does not exist, throw an IllegalArgumentException.
```

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  1.3 Locate these files in your project.

4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   * Summary: Calculates the sum of the budget amounts of all projects across all departments in the company.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Initialize total = 0.0
   *   2. For each department in departments:
   *      a. For each project in department.projects:
   *         - Add project.budget to total
   *   3. Return total

   * @generated NOT
   */
  public double calculateTotalBudget()
  {
    double total = 0.0;
    for (Department department : getDepartments())

   * Post-condition: Returns the number of distinct employees involved in production projects.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int countEmployeesInProductionProjects()
  {
    Set<Employee> productionEmployees = new HashSet<Employee>();
    for (Department department : getDepartments())
    {
      for (Project project : department.getProjects())
      {
        if (project instanceof ProductionProject)
        {
          for (Employee employee : project.getWorkingEmployees())
          {
            productionEmployees.add(employee);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return productionEmployees.size();
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Adds a new department to the company, ensuring the company always maintains between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive).
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Validate the input department name is not null, empty, or duplicate.
   *   2. Check that the current number of departments is less than 8.
   *   3. If validation fails, throw appropriate exception.
   *   4. Add the department to the departments list.
   *   5. Set the department's company reference to this company.
   * Related Features: departments (List), company reference of Department
   * Related Classes: Department
   * Input:
   *   - department: Department object to be added. Must have a non-null, non-empty, unique name.
   * Output: void
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - department is not null
   *   - department.name is not null and not empty
   *   - department.name is unique among existing departments
   *   - departments.size() < 8
   * Post-condition:
   *   - departments.size() increases by 1
   *   - The new department is added to departments list
   *   - department.company == this
   * Exceptions:
   *   - IllegalArgumentException: if department is null, name is null/empty, or name is duplicate
   *   - IllegalStateException: if departments.size() >= 8
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public void addDepartment(final Department department)
  {
    if (department == null)
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("department must not be null");
    }
    String name = department.getID();
    if (name == null || name.isEmpty())
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("department name must not be null or empty");
    }
    for (Department existing : getDepartments())
    {
      if (name.equals(existing.getID()))
      {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("department name must be unique");
      }
    }
    if (getDepartments().size() >= 8)
    {
      throw new IllegalStateException("company cannot have more than 8 departments");
    }
    getDepartments().add(department);
    department.setCompany(this);
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Removes an existing department by name from the company, ensuring the company always maintains between 2 and 8 departments (inclusive).
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Validate name is not null or empty.
   *   2. Find the department with matching name in departments list.
   *   3. If not found, throw IllegalArgumentException.
   *   4. Check that departments.size() > 2 (after removal would be at least 2).
   *   5. If validation fails, throw appropriate exception.
   *   6. Remove the department from departments list.
   *   7. Return the name of the removed department.
   * Related Features: departments (List)
   * Related Classes: Department
   * Input:
   *   - name: String - The name (ID) of the department to remove. Must not be null or empty.
   * Output: String - The name of the department that was removed.
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - name is not null and not empty
   *   - A department with the given name exists in departments
   *   - departments.size() > 2
   * Post-condition:
   *   - departments.size() decreases by 1
   *   - The department with the given name is removed
   *   - Returns the name of the removed department
   * Exceptions:
   *   - IllegalArgumentException: if name is null/empty, or department not found
   *   - IllegalStateException: if departments.size() <= 2
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public String removeDepartment(final String name)
  {
    if (name == null || name.isEmpty())
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("name must not be null or empty");
    }
    Department found = null;
    for (Department department : getDepartments())
    {
      if (name.equals(department.getID()))
      {
        found = department;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (found == null)
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("department not found: " + name);
    }
    if (getDepartments().size() <= 2)
    {
      throw new IllegalStateException("company must have at least 2 departments");
    }
    getDepartments().remove(found);
    return name;
  }

- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

   * Summary: Calculates the average budget amount of all projects in this department.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If projects list is empty, return 0.0
   *   2. Initialize sum = 0.0
   *   3. For each project in projects:
   *      a. Add project.budget to sum
   *   4. Return sum / projects.size()
   * Related Features: projects (List), budget (in Project)
   * Related Classes: Project
   * Input: None
   * Output: double - The average budget of all projects in this department. Returns 0.0 if no projects exist.
   * Pre-condition: None
   * Post-condition: Returns the arithmetic mean of all project budgets in this department.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public double calculateAverageBudget()
  {
    EList<Project> projectsList = getProjects();
    if (projectsList.isEmpty())
    {
      return 0.0;
    }
    double sum = 0.0;
    for (Project project : projectsList)
    {
      sum += project.getBudget();
    }
    return sum / projectsList.size();
  }
